| ❌ Neon purple/blue linear gradient cards | ✅ Deep Zinc/Slate neutral surfaces with 1px micro-borders |
| ❌ Cluttered `rounded-3xl` bubbly shapes | ✅ Refined 8pt grid, optical kerning & Geist/SF Pro typography |

p99 latency spikes and parameter sniffing degradation), the "Composite Index Column Misplacement & Cartesian Descent Trap" (where placing array columns before high-cardinality equality prefixes breaks index clustering and multiplies leaf-page traversals exponentially), and the "Un-Deduplicated Client Array & Cache-Line Thrashing Trap" across autonomous coding agents (Claude Code, Cursor Composer, - ⚡ **Agentic Database Extended Query Protocol, libpq Wire Pipelining, Binary Format Encoding & Round-Trip Latency (RTT) Defense (`rules/agentic-database-extended-query-protocol-pipelining-binary-format-rtt-defense.md`)**: Complete architectural framework eliminating the "Simple Query Protocol Re-Parsing & SQL Injection Trap" numbers, UUIDs, timestamps, and vector embeddings in Text Format `format=0` instead of native Binary Format `format=1` burns 35%–50% of application and database CPU cycles in string parsing, message, forcing PostgreSQL to perform intermediate snapshot cleanup, WAL flush evaluations, and context switches; whereas wire pipelining emits multiple `Parse`/`Bind`/`Execute` messages followed by ban on Simple Query `'Q'` in production OLTP, parameterized templates `$1, $2`, and statement cache pooling $\to$ Tier 1 Native Binary Wire Format Encoding: `format=1` for UUIDs, timestamps, 64-bit into a single TCP socket flight with one terminal `Sync`, collapsing RTT from $O(N) \to O(1)$ $\to$ Tier 3 Resilient Pipeline Error Handling with Savepoint Fencing: isolating row failures with `SAVEPOINT sp_k` to prevent `PGRES_PIPELINE_ABORTED` cascades $\to$ Tier 4 Kernel Socket Optimization: explicit `TCP_NODELAY = 1`, `SO_SNDBUF` / `SO_RCVBUF` tuning, and PgBouncer 1.21+ pipeline OpenCode, SWE-bench), establishing a strict 5-Tier BRIN & Time-Series Governance Ladder (Tier 0 Physical Correlation Gate & Anti-Random Sentinels: verifying $|\text{correlation}| \ge 0.95$ in batch streaming and zero in-place `UPDATE` mutations on append-only relations, Tier 2 Calibrated `pages_per_range` Sizing & `autosummarize = on` Invariance: tuning `pages_per_range = 16-64` based on - 🪣 **Cloud Object Storage, S3 & Blob Storage Invariants (`rules/cloud-object-storage-s3.md`)**: Direct-to-storage presigned upload architecture bypassing web server proxy bottlenecks, strict presigned URL expiration ($\le 15\text{m}$) and policy condition constraints (`content-length-range`, exact MIME `Content-Type`), multi-tenant key namespacing with UUIDv7 collision prevention (`tenants/{tenant_id}/{resource}/{uuidv7}.ext`), the multi-thousand dollar incomplete multipart upload leak and mandatory `AbortIncompleteMultipartUpload` automated lifecycle purge rules ($\le 7\text{ days}$), multipart part sizing invariants (5MB min, 10,000 part ceiling, 64MB–512MB scaling for multi-gigabyte files), the multipart ETag mismatch pitfall and modern S3 flexible checksums (streaming hardware-accelerated CRC32C and SHA-256 trailing checksum verification), strong read-after-write consistency nuances and S3 Conditional Writes (`If-None-Match: *` to prevent accidental last-writer-wins race overwrites; `If-Match: "<etag>"` for optimistic updates), zero-egress economics via free AWS S3 Gateway VPC Endpoints (eliminating the $0.045/GB NAT Gateway data transfer fee) and Cloudflare R2 zero-egress asset distribution, event-driven S3 pipeline race condition defense via immutable `versionId` targeting, byte-range requests (`Range: bytes=start-end`) for media and Parquet/DuckDB query pushdown, safe pre-flight metadata inspection (`HeadObject` with 404 handling), security hardening via 4-point S3 Block Public Access, disabling legacy ACLs (`BucketOwnerEnforced`), SSE-KMS cost reduction via S3 Bucket Keys (`bucket_key_enabled = true`), WORM Object Lock (Compliance Mode vs Governance Mode) against ransomware, and high-throughput partition prefix scaling (3,500 PUT / 5,500 GET req/s per prefix).
- 🏢 **Multi-Tenant SaaS Architecture, Data Isolation & RLS Invariants (`rules/multi-tenant-saas-architecture.md`)**: Architectural trade-offs across tenancy models (Pooled / Shared Schema vs Bridge / Schema-per-tenant vs Silo / DB-per-tenant) and the enterprise hybrid routing tier, PostgreSQL Row-Level Security (RLS) enforcement and the table owner bypass vulnerability (mandatory `FORCE ROW LEVEL SECURITY` and safe `current_setting('app.current_tenant_id', true)` null checks), connection pooling isolation traps with PgBouncer transaction pooling mode (strict prohibition of session-level `SET app.current_tenant_id`; mandatory transaction-scoped `SET LOCAL` within `BEGIN ... COMMIT` blocks or context wrappers to eliminate cross-tenant pooled connection pollution), Broken Object Level Authorization (BOLA / IDOR) defense with composite primary keys (`PRIMARY KEY (tenant_id, id)`) and dual-scoping application queries, cryptographic JWT tenant claim (`tid`) verification and DNS subdomain resolution discipline, noisy neighbor defense via weighted fair-share queue dispatching (round-robin tenant workers with active worker caps to prevent interactive starvation), hierarchical cache key namespacing (`tenant:{tenant_id}:{resource}:{id}`) and instant tenant-wide cache invalidation via cache version counters, sliding-window Redis Lua rate limiters partitioned by tenant pricing tier, two-phase tenant data shredding for GDPR Right to be Forgotten compliance (immediate access revocation + chunked batch background deletion loops yielding I/O), and tamper-proof multi-tenant audit logs with HMAC entry hash chaining.
- 💳 **Financial Ledgers, Payment Systems & Double-Entry Invariants (`rules/financial-ledgers-payment-systems.md`)**: Absolute ban on floating-point arithmetic for currency (mandatory ISO 4217 integer minor currency units: cents/yen/fils) and Banker's Rounding (Round Half to Even IEEE 754) to eliminate statistical accumulation bias, formal double-entry bookkeeping equation ($\text{Assets} + \text{Expenses} = \text{Liabilities} + \text{Equity} + \text{Revenue}$) with deferred database triggers enforcing $\sum \text{Debits} - \sum \text{Credits} = 0$, immutable append-only journal entries (ban on `UPDATE`/`DELETE` mutations; all adjustments via reversing entries), atomic balance updates with non-negative guard conditions (`WHERE balance >= :debit_amount`) and deadlock-free deterministic lexicographical lock ordering (`SELECT FOR UPDATE WHERE id IN (...) ORDER BY id ASC`), two-phase payment lifecycles (separate Authorize & Capture flows to eliminate fulfillment refunds and merchant interchange fees), webhook security invariants (mandatory untouched raw buffer HMAC-SHA256 verification before JSON parsing, timing-safe constant-time comparison via `crypto.timingSafeEqual`, and 300s replay attack timestamp tolerance), pre-persistence discipline (pre-persisting local payment intent and order before calling external gateways to eliminate orphaned gateway charges upon database network drops), transactional outbox pattern for financial event emission, automated daily three-way match reconciliation engines (Gateway Settlement Reports vs Internal Ledgers vs Bank Payouts), and PCI-DSS Level 1 zero-PAN invariants (tokenization via client-side hosted fields and Luhn regex log redaction).
- 🧬 **Vector Databases, Embeddings & Semantic Retrieval (`rules/vector-databases-embeddings.md`)**: Pre-normalized unit vector invariants ($\|\mathbf{v}\|_2 = 1.0$) and dot product / inner product (`<#>`, `ip`) SIMD acceleration over unnormalized cosine distance square root calculations, HNSW graph topology parameter tuning ($M$, $\text{ef\_construction}$, $\text{ef\_search}$) with strict working-set memory footprint sizing ($\text{RAM} \approx N \times (\text{dim} \times 4 + M \times 16) \times 1.25$) to prevent disk paging and build-time OOM crashes, the pre-filtering vs post-filtering disaster (mandatory payload schema inverted indexing in Qdrant/pgvector to eliminate post-filter recall collapse and unindexed sequential full scans), Scalar Quantization (SQ8 / int8) with $3 \times$ oversampling and rescoring for 75% RAM savings at >99% recall, pgvector 0.7+ `halfvec` (FP16) storage optimization, bulk load before indexing discipline (`COPY` before `CREATE INDEX` with `maintenance_work_mem = '4GB'` and `max_parallel_maintenance_workers = 4`), PostgreSQL HNSW MVCC bloat defense decoupling immutable vector tables from volatile metadata, embedding model provenance tracking and dimension invariance, asymmetric retrieval prompt prefixes (`passage: ` vs `query: ` in E5/BGE/Nomic), hybrid search combining dense vectors and lexical BM25/`tsvector` via Reciprocal Rank Fusion (RRF $k=60$), two-stage retrieval with Cross-Encoder reranking (Cohere Rerank / BGE-Reranker-Large), and multi-tenant declarative list partitioning with localized HNSW graphs.
- 🕸️ **GraphQL Schema Architecture, Apollo Federation v2 & Supergraphs (`rules/graphql-apollo-federation.md`)**: The N+1 query catastrophe & DataLoader request-scoped batching invariant (mandatory fresh `createDataLoaders()` per request context; ban on module-level loaders leaking tenant data across requests), schema resilience & the partial failure nullability contract (semantic nullability, resilient relations `[Item!]` to isolate downstream service degradation without root query nullification, reserving non-null `!` strictly for IDs and mandatory mutation inputs), resilient mutation design with typed `userErrors: [UserError!]!` payloads (ban on raw `GraphQLError` exceptions for business logic), Denial-of-Service defense via static AST query depth limiting (max depth 6–10) and complexity scoring (field weights and list multipliers), production hardening via disabled introspection in production, Automatic Persisted Queries (APQ with SHA-256) and operation safelisting, Apollo Federation v2 supergraph architecture (`@key`, `@shareable`, `@inaccessible`, `@override`) with batched `__resolveReference` entity resolvers, field-level authorization and Broken Object Level Authorization (BOLA/IDOR) tenant scoping guards, and modern `graphql-ws` WebSocket subscriptions with keep-alive pings and generator `finally` cleanup.
- 🌐 **NGINX, Reverse Proxies & Ingress Gateway Invariants (NGINX, OpenResty, Caddy & Traefik) (`rules/nginx-reverse-proxy.md`)**: Off-by-slash path traversal security defense (mandatory trailing slash alignment on `location` and `alias`), `proxy_pass` URI rewriting traps (transparent forwarding vs prefix stripping), upstream keepalive connection pooling (`proxy_http_version 1.1; proxy_set_header Connection ""; keepalive 64;`) to eliminate `TIME_WAIT` TCP socket exhaustion, dynamic DNS resolution in cloud/container environments (runtime variable upstream assignment with explicit `resolver ... valid=10s`), WebSocket dynamic hop-by-hop upgrade mapping (`map $http_upgrade $connection_upgrade`), Server-Sent Events (SSE) & LLM token streaming optimization (`proxy_buffering off; chunked_transfer_encoding on;`), real client IP restoration and anti-spoofing defense (`set_real_ip_from`, `real_ip_header`, `real_ip_recursive on`), in-memory proxy buffer tuning (`proxy_buffers 8 32k; proxy_busy_buffers_size 64k;`) to prevent disk spooling thrashing, client body payload ceilings (`client_max_body_size`), production rate limiting with `$binary_remote_addr`, `burst`, `nodelay` and HTTP 429 status, modern TLS/SSL hardening (TLS 1.2/1.3 exclusively, AEAD ciphers, OCSP stapling, HSTS `preload`, `server_tokens off;`), zero-downtime atomic test-and-reload protocol (`nginx -t && nginx -s reload`), and SPA routing fallback hygiene.
- 🔍 **Search Engines & Inverted Index Invariants (Elasticsearch, OpenSearch & Meilisearch) (`rules/search-elasticsearch-meilisearch.md`)**: Zero-downtime index migrations and mandatory dual-alias abstraction (`products_search` for reads and `products_write` for writes with atomic `_aliases` switching), explicit mapping discipline with total ban on unconstrained dynamic mapping (`dynamic: strict` / `dynamic: false` to eliminate cluster-wide mapping explosions and master JVM heap crashes), strict `text` (analyzed full-text search) vs `keyword` (exact filter, sort, aggregations) field segregation and total ban on in-memory Fielddata, deep pagination defense (ban on `from + size > 10,000` memory spikes; mandatory Point-in-Time `_pit` and `search_after` with unique tie-breaker), Bulk API batch sizing invariants (5MB–15MB / 1,000–5,000 docs) with backpressure handling (HTTP 429 `TOO_MANY_REQUESTS` / `EsRejectedExecutionException` jittered retry loops), shard sizing rules (10GB–30GB for search, 30GB–50GB for time-series) and custom `routing` keys for multi-tenant isolation (eliminating cluster-wide scatter-gather network fanout), filter context (64-bit Roaring Bitmaps cache) vs query context, leading wildcard ban (`*term*` sequential dictionary scan defense), hybrid search via dense vector HNSW embeddings and BM25 lexical retrieval merged with Reciprocal Rank Fusion (`rrf`), and Meilisearch production invariants (explicit `searchableAttributes`, `filterableAttributes`, `sortableAttributes`, alphanumeric document IDs, and async task queue `waitForTask` synchronization).
- 📦 **Monorepo Architecture, Workspace Boundaries & Build Graph Invariants (`rules/monorepo-workspaces.md`)**: Package manager purity (enforce `only-allow pnpm` and zero tolerance for foreign lockfile pollution), root vs package dependency segregation (runtime dependencies strictly in package manifests; root reserved for repo orchestration), internal package linking via `workspace:*` protocol, total ban on cross-package relative path deep imports (`import { x } from '../../packages/core/src/internal/...'`), modern package.json `exports` map encapsulation (types, import, subpath routing), acyclic dependency graph (DAG) enforcement (`madge --circular`), TypeScript Project References architecture (`composite: true`, `declarationMap: true` for direct source navigation, root `tsc --build` orchestration), Turborepo/Nx pipeline task caching (`dependsOn: ["^build"]`, strict `inputs`, `.env*` variable declaration in `env` to eliminate remote cache poisoning), agent context economy via filtered execution (`pnpm --filter <pkg>...`, `turbo run test --filter=...[origin/main]`), Changesets intent-driven semantic versioning (`.changeset/`), and dual ESM/CJS `tsup` bundling standards.
- 🗃️ **SQLite, DuckDB & Embedded In-Process Storage (`rules/sqlite-duckdb-embedded.md`)**: Mandatory production PRAGMA tuning sequence (`journal_mode = WAL`, `synchronous = NORMAL`, `busy_timeout = 5000`, `foreign_keys = ON`, `cache_size = -64000`, `temp_store = MEMORY`, `mmap_size = 256MB`), concurrency architecture invariants (unlimited parallel readers with strictly single-writer lock discipline), total ban on deferred `BEGIN` transaction upgrade deadlocks (`BEGIN IMMEDIATE` invariant), SQLite 3.37+ `STRICT` table schema enforcement, ISO-8601 UTC string datetime standard, stored vs virtual generated columns with JSON extraction indexes, external content FTS5 full-text search tables with lightweight sync triggers (`AFTER INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE`), BM25 relevance ranking with column weighting and substring trigram tokenizers, DuckDB vectorized in-process OLAP engine pushdown across multi-gigabyte Parquet/CSV files (zero Python/memory load overhead), host process memory limits (`SET max_memory = '4GB'`) and disk spill directories against OOM crashes, zero-copy Apache Arrow RecordBatch exchange, direct SQLite database attachment (`ATTACH ... AS sqlite_db`), the 12-step zero-downtime table recreation migration protocol, non-blocking online live backups (`VACUUM INTO`), WAL checkpointing and incremental vacuuming, and local-first client UUID v7 generation for collision-free edge synchronization (Turso/libSQL & Cloudflare D1).
- 📊 **ClickHouse & Real-Time Columnar OLAP Analytics (`rules/clickhouse-olap.md`)**: Columnar storage execution model, MergeTree family engine selection (`ReplacingMergeTree`, `VersionedCollapsingMergeTree`, `AggregatingMergeTree`), total ban on single-row OLTP-style inserts, write batching invariants (10,000–100,000 rows or server-side `async_insert=1` buffers) to eliminate the fatal `Too many parts` crash, coarse-grained monthly partition key pruning (`toYYYYMM`) with <1,000 active partition ceiling, `ORDER BY` physical sorting and sparse primary index marks (low to high cardinality column ordering), `SELECT ... FINAL` performance degradation defense with vectorized `argMax` deduplication, ban on heavy asynchronous `ALTER TABLE ... UPDATE/DELETE` mutations in hot paths, automatic TTL purges, `LowCardinality(String)` 85% disk compression and integer filtering, avoidance of `Nullable(T)` overhead, streaming insert-time Materialized Views with `AggregatingMergeTree` (`-State` and `-Merge` combinators), query memory ceilings (`max_memory_usage` and external disk spill), `GLOBAL JOIN` broadcast against multi-node network fanout, and HyperLogLog approximation (`uniq` / `uniqCombined64`).
- 💻 **Shell Scripting, Bash Automation & POSIX CLI Discipline (`rules/shell-bash-cli.md`)**: Mandatory strict mode (`set -euo pipefail` and `IFS=$'\n\t'`), total ban on unquoted variables to prevent word splitting and glob expansion, the `local` declaration exit code masking trap (separate `local x` from assignment `x=$(cmd)`), self-resolving deterministic script location (`BASH_SOURCE[0]`), atomic temporary workspaces with `mktemp -d` and deterministic signal cleanup traps (`trap cleanup EXIT INT TERM`), atomic file updates (`mv -f temp dest`), safe stream processing with `while IFS= read -r line`, catastrophic recursive deletion defense (`rm -rf "${DIR:?}"/*` with empty variable protection), stdout (machine data) vs stderr (human diagnostics) stream separation, portable binary discovery (`command -v` over `which`), and non-interactive CLI flags for automation.
- ⚡ **Edge Computing, Cloudflare Workers & Serverless Runtimes (`rules/edge-serverless-workers.md`)**: Ephemeral V8 isolate execution model and strict ban on mutable module-scope global state (prevents multi-tenant isolate cross-request data leaks), mandatory `ctx.waitUntil(promise)` lifecycle preservation for post-response async operations, zero-native C++ WinterCG boundary enforcement, relational database connection exhaustion defense via Cloudflare Hyperdrive and serverless HTTP drivers, Durable Objects actor consistency with atomic multi-key `storage.transaction()`, zero-cost WebSocket Hibernation API (`ctx.acceptWebSocket()`), storage tier invariants (Workers KV eventual consistency vs D1 relational SQLite vs R2 zero-egress object storage), 128MB memory ceiling streaming defense (`ReadableStream` / `TransformStream`), 50-subrequest budget mitigation, Smart Placement cross-continental latency elimination, and encrypted Wrangler secrets.
- ⚙️ **Distributed Background Jobs, Task Queues & Durable Workflows (`rules/background-jobs-workflows.md`)**: Universal at-least-once delivery reality and mandatory handler idempotency keys, payload minimalism (pass lightweight IDs, never live database entities), exponential backoff with full jitter to eliminate retry storm thundering herds, Dead Letter Queue (DLQ) poison pill isolation, Temporal/Cadence/Inngest durable execution determinism (ban on `time.Now()`, `Math.random()`, direct network I/O, or raw goroutines inside workflows; delegate all non-deterministic logic to activities), worker graceful shutdown (`SIGTERM`) with in-flight drain and heartbeat timeouts, multi-tenant fair queuing against starvation, and incremental batch checkpointing.
- 🧠 **Agent Context Engineering, Memory Architecture & KV Cache Optimization (`rules/context-memory-engineering.md`)**: Strict Prompt Prefix KV Caching determinism for 10x latency speedups and 75-90% cost savings (ban on volatile timestamps/metrics before prompt prefix), 3-tier progressive disclosure hierarchy (AST/symbol discovery -> targeted line slices -> complete file reads only on structural refactors), persistent memory bank protocols (`ACTIVE_CONTEXT.md` / `SYSTEM_PATTERNS.md`), sub-agent isolation with 5-line synthesized handoff blocks to prevent orchestrator context rot, semantic delimiter containment (`<file_content>`, `<tool_output>`) against indirect prompt injection, and the 3-attempt circuit breaker against speculative thrashing.
- 🔐 **Authentication, OAuth 2.1 & Identity Security (`rules/auth-identity.md`)**: Absolute ban on `localStorage` / `sessionStorage` token storage (prevents XSS exfiltration), hardened `__Host-` / `HttpOnly` / `SameSite=Lax/Strict` cookie enforcement, JWT algorithm pinning (eliminates `alg: none` and RS256/HS256 public key confusion exploits), refresh token rotation (RTR) with automatic token family breach invalidation, mandatory OAuth 2.1 PKCE (`S256`), CSRF `state` and OIDC `nonce` validation, exact-match redirect URI whitelisting, Broken Object Level Authorization (BOLA/IDOR) tenant scoping guards, and WebAuthn/Passkeys monotonic signature counter verification.

- 🛡️ **Application Security & OWASP Defense (`rules/security-appsec.md`)**: Anti-SSRF guards with DNS pre-flight & private IP bans, absolute command injection / shell interpolation elimination, parameterized SQL invariants, path traversal immunity with canonical base checks, constant-time cryptographic comparison (`timingSafeEqual`), CSPRNG token discipline, and ReDoS prevention.
- 🐳 **Docker & Container Production Invariants (`rules/docker-containers.md`)**: Multi-stage build isolation with minimal attack surfaces (`distroless`/`alpine`), absolute ban on running as root (`USER nonroot`), PID 1 signal forwarding (`tini`/exec form) for zero-downtime graceful shutdown, secret isolation via `--mount=type=secret`, same-layer package cache pruning, and deterministic healthchecks.
- ☸️ **Kubernetes & Cloud-Native Infrastructure (`rules/kubernetes-cloud-native.md`)**: Absolute ban on external dependency checks in `livenessProbe` (preventing cascading cluster death spirals), mandatory `preStop` sleep hooks to eliminate 502 errors during rolling deployments, `terminationGracePeriodSeconds` sizing, Pod Security Standards (PSS) restricted hardening (`runAsNonRoot: true`, `readOnlyRootFilesystem: true`, drop all capabilities), `immutable: true` ConfigMaps/Secrets, secret volume mounts over environment variables, and PodDisruptionBudgets (PDB).
- 🏗️ **Terraform & Infrastructure as Code (IaC) (`rules/terraform-iac.md`)**: Remote state locking with DynamoDB/S3 encryption, mandatory `lifecycle { prevent_destroy = true }` on production stateful resources, `create_before_destroy = true` for zero-downtime resource replacement, absolute ban on IAM wildcard `Action: "*"` and `Resource: "*"` statements, `0.0.0.0/0` ingress prohibition on non-web ports, mandatory S3 4-point public access blocks and SSE-KMS encryption, dynamic secret injection over plaintext `.tfvars`, and pessimistic provider version pinning (`~> 5.40`).
- 🔌 **Model Context Protocol (MCP) Server Engineering (`rules/mcp-server.md`)**: Absolute ban on stdio `stdout` pollution (`console.log` / `print` breaks JSON-RPC byte framing), mandatory descriptive Zod/Pydantic schemas for LLM tool selection, bounded token payload ceilings with pagination/projections, graceful error handling (`isError: true`), path traversal / shell injection immunity, dynamic tool federation & prefix namespacing against collision in multi-server gateways, incremental output streaming via `notifications/message`, session boundary & tenant isolation in remote SSE gateways, progress notifications (`notifications/progress`) for long-running tools, contextual argument tab autocompletion (`completion/complete`), and multi-modal image/binary content payload formatting.

- ⚡ **Redis & In-Memory Caching Invariants (`rules/redis-caching.md`)**: Absolute ban on `KEYS *` in production (enforces non-blocking `SCAN` cursors), `UNLINK` over synchronous `DEL` for BigKeys, cache stampede / thundering herd mitigation via Singleflight and probabilistic early expiration (XFetch), mandatory TTL jitter to eliminate cache avalanches, cache penetration defense (sentinel empty caching & Bloom filters), safe distributed locking with atomic token verification via Lua scripts, sliding-window rate limiters with Sorted Sets, and cache-aside invalidation discipline.
- 📨 **Apache Kafka & Event Streaming Invariants (`rules/kafka-event-streaming.md`)**: Mandatory idempotent producer (`enable.idempotence=true`), strict `acks=all` with `min.insync.replicas=2` delivery guarantees, partition key strategies to eliminate hot partitions, consumer poll loop heartbeat safety (`max.poll.interval.ms` starvation prevention), prohibition of auto-commit (`enable.auto.commit=false`) to prevent silent data loss, `CooperativeStickyAssignor` to eradicate stop-the-world rebalance storms, three-tier non-blocking retries with Dead Letter Topics (DLT), Schema Registry backward/forward compatibility contracts, and signal-driven graceful shutdown with consumer `wakeup()`.
- 📊 **Observability, OpenTelemetry & Telemetry Hygiene (`rules/observability-telemetry.md`)**: Absolute ban on raw unformatted log statements, structured JSON logging with automated PII and credential masking, Prometheus metric cardinality explosion defense (strict ban on dynamic user/order IDs in labels), metric type semantics (Counter, Gauge, Histogram buckets), the Four Golden Signals, W3C `traceparent` distributed context propagation, OpenTelemetry span lifecycle management with explicit error status tagging, and probe decoupling (`/livez` vs `/readyz`).
- ⚡ **gRPC & Protocol Buffers Production Invariants (`rules/grpc-protobuf.md`)**: Binary wire compatibility guarantees, immutable field tag numbering, mandatory `reserved` tagging upon field deprecation, `ENUM_TYPE_UNSPECIFIED = 0` standard to prevent zero-value semantic ambiguity, proto3 `optional` presence flags, W3C `grpc-timeout` deadline propagation, canonical gRPC status code mapping (`codes.InvalidArgument`, `codes.NotFound`, `codes.FailedPrecondition`), rich error details with `google.rpc.Status` (`BadRequest.FieldViolation`), streaming backpressure safety, singleton `ClientConn` reuse, NAT/load balancer keepalive ping settings (`KeepaliveEnforcementPolicy`), and layered interceptor ordering.
- 📡 **WebSockets, Server-Sent Events & Real-Time Streaming (`rules/websockets-realtime.md`)**: Architectural protocol matrix (SSE for unidirectional LLM token/progress streams vs WebSockets for duplex collaborative editing), absolute ban on short-polling, native Ping/Pong control frame heartbeats and dead TCP connection pruning, reconnection storm mitigation via full-jitter exponential backoff, monotonic message IDs with server replay ring buffers for gapless reconnection, outbound backpressure with slow consumer eviction to prevent server OOM, multi-node horizontal fanout via distributed pub/sub brokers (Redis Streams/NATS), zero-downtime rolling deploys with WebSocket Close Code `1001 (Going Away)`, and Cross-Site WebSocket Hijacking (CSWSH) Origin whitelist validation.
- ⚡ **Web Performance, Core Web Vitals & Browser Runtime Invariants (`rules/web-performance-vitals.md`)**: Absolute ban on blocking long tasks (>50ms) in event handlers (Interaction to Next Paint / INP < 200ms threshold), yielding to paint via `scheduler.yield()` / macrotask fallback, layout thrashing & forced synchronous reflow elimination (batching reads before writes in `requestAnimationFrame`), Cumulative Layout Shift (CLS < 0.1) defense with mandatory media dimensions and font metric matching (`size-adjust`), Largest Contentful Paint (LCP < 2.5s) hero image priority (`fetchpriority="high"`, `loading="eager"`), DOM tree ceiling (<1,500 nodes) with list virtualization, dynamic code splitting for heavy dependencies, and detached DOM memory leak elimination.
- 💚 **Vue 3 & Nuxt 3 (`rules/vue-nuxt.md`)**: Enforces `<script setup lang="ts">`, Vue 3.4+ `defineModel()` declarative two-way binding invariants, Vue 3.5+ `useId()` hydration-safe accessible ID generation, reactive props destructuring & `useTemplateRef()`, `onWatcherCleanup()` async race cancellation, composable argument normalization with `toValue` & `MaybeRefOrGetter`, deterministic SSR hydration hygiene, bans `reactive()` reassignment, strictly bans module-scope mutable state in Nuxt SSR (prevents multi-tenant cross-request state pollution), enforces Nitro runtime validation (`readValidatedBody`) and server middleware memory leak defense, server endpoint caching and multi-tenant isolation with `defineCachedEventHandler` (`getKey` tenant scoping), Nuxt 3 route middleware navigation return discipline (`return navigateTo(...)`), guarantees Pinia `storeToRefs`, `$patch` atomic state mutation batching, and `onScopeDispose` composable event cleanup, `v-memo` Virtual DOM subtree optimization for high-density virtualized rows, accessible headless component primitives (Radix Vue / Reka UI) with keyboard focus traps, Teleport client-mounted guards & SSR hydration safety, Nuxt image optimization, optimistic UI updates with rollback, `shallowRef` and `markRaw` memory overhead optimization for massive collections and third-party editor/canvas instances, `<NuxtErrorBoundary>` and `onErrorCaptured` lifecycle error boundaries, Nuxt custom payload serializers with `definePayloadReducer` and `definePayloadReviver`, SSR safe browser API guards (`import.meta.client` & `onMounted`), `useAsyncData` parameterized unique cache key deduplication, post-flush DOM layout measurement (`watchPostEffect` / `{ flush: 'post' }`), reusable performance debouncing with Vue `customRef`, Nuxt 3 `useCookie` security hardening (`httpOnly`, `secure`, `sameSite`) and cookie chunking defense for tokens > 4KB, composable memory leak defense with `onScopeDispose` for window listeners and WebSockets, `shallowRef` & `markRaw` for heavy external instances (Canvas, Three.js, Monaco, WebGL), Nitro server route error sanitization via `createError`, SSR hydration-safe deterministic timestamps, Vue 3.5+ `watch` depth limiting (`deep: 1/2`) against recursive proxy walks, `useFetch` vs `$fetch` event callback discipline, Vue 3.5+ watcher controls (`pause()` and `resume()`) for batch updates without reactive feedback storms, Nuxt 3 SSR canonical host header poisoning defense, Nuxt 3 hybrid rendering & `routeRules` caching discipline (`isr` vs `swr`), Pinia `$onAction` & `$subscribe` lifecycle detachment defense, Vue 3.5+ `useTemplateRef` typed instance refs, and Nitro server storage via Unstorage multi-environment drivers.
- 🧡 **Svelte 5 Runes & SvelteKit (`rules/svelte-sveltekit.md`)**: Complete ban on legacy Svelte 3/4 reactive syntax, strict `$state` vs `$state.raw` memory discipline, Svelte 5 `$derived.by()` multi-statement derivations, Svelte 5 `$bindable()` runes two-way contract, prohibition of `$effect()` for derived computations, strict ban on synchronous `$state` mutations inside `$effect` (preventing cascading reactive loops), pre-paint DOM measurements via `$effect.pre()`, intentional dependency exclusion via `untrack()`, strict SvelteKit server security boundaries (`+page.server.ts` vs universal `+page.ts`), SSR hydration guards, form actions progressive enhancement (`use:enhance`) with CSRF protection and accessible `fail(400, ...)` validation errors, universal reactivity safety in `.svelte.ts` modules with Svelte 5 class instances, accessible headless widget primitives (Bits UI), View Transitions API integration, compile-time image optimization (`@sveltejs/enhanced-img`), optimistic mutations with automated error rollbacks, granular URL / dependency cache invalidation (`invalidate(url)` over `invalidateAll()`), Svelte 5 typed Snippets (`{#snippet}` & `{@render}`) over deprecated legacy slots, typed callback props in `$props()` over deprecated `createEventDispatcher` / `on:click`, internal service auth & request rewriting via `handleFetch` server hook, transient UI & scroll preservation with SvelteKit `snapshot` API, streaming non-blocking deferred promises in SvelteKit `load` for instant sub-second TTFB, SvelteKit Server-Sent Events (SSE) streaming pattern with native `ReadableStream` and request abort cancellation, Svelte 5 `$inspect` rune with zero production bundle overhead (`$inspect(...).with(...)`), SvelteKit Form Actions with `use:enhance` optimistic pending state UI lockouts against double-submits, SvelteKit `sequence` modular server middleware composition (`hooks.server.ts`), Svelte 5 deep reactive proxy mutation discipline, Svelte 5 `$derived.by()` multi-statement logic, SvelteKit semantic `depends()` / `invalidate()` cache hygiene, SvelteKit `data-sveltekit-preload-data="hover"` vs `"tap"` memory and bandwidth scaling invariants, Svelte 5 recursive snippet depth guards against call stack overflow, Svelte 5 `$effect.tracking()` reactivity detection, SvelteKit `use:enhance` form submission lifecycle & input preservation on failure, Svelte 5 Snippets composition & slot elimination discipline, and SvelteKit server load parallelization with deferred streaming.
- ⚛️ **Next.js 14/15 & React (`rules/react-nextjs.md`)**: Enforces App Router Server/Client boundaries, Next.js 15 async request APIs (`await params`), `server-only` leak protection, Server Actions authorization guards, hydration mismatch elimination, Server Secret leak prevention via React Taint APIs (`experimental_taintUniqueValue` / `experimental_taintObjectReference`), non-blocking transitions with `useTransition`, and tactile aesthetic restraint.
- 🦀 **Rust Industrial Safety (`rules/rust.md`)**: Strictly bans `.unwrap()` in production branches, enforces `Result<?>` error propagation, Tokio async executor starvation guards, and zero-copy borrowing patterns.
- 🐍 **Python & FastAPI (`rules/python-fastapi.md`)**: Mandates strict type hints, Pydantic validation, and bans silent `except Exception: pass`.
- 🤖 **Multi-Agent & MCP Protocols (`templates/AGENTS.md`)**: Context window anti-rot discipline, compacted hand-offs, sub-agent isolation, and safe Model Context Protocol (MCP) tool payload constraints.
- 🎯 **Modern Cursor `.mdc` Rules**: All rules include YAML frontmatter (`description`, `globs`, `alwaysApply: false`) ready for Cursor's intelligent auto-attaching rule engine.

---

## ⚡ 1-Second Quick Start

Drop into your current project with one command:

```bash
curl -fsSL https://raw.githubusercontent.com/TrustDec/agent-arsenal/main/install.sh | bash
```

Or copy the files directly into your project root:
- Copy `rules/CLAUDE.md` to `./CLAUDE.md` (and `./.windsurfrules`, `./.clinerules`, `./.github/copilot-instructions.md`)
- Copy `rules/.cursorrules` to `./.cursorrules` (and stack rules into `./.cursor/rules/*.mdc`)
- Copy `skills/` to `./.agents/skills/`
- Copy `templates/AGENTS.md` to `./AGENTS.md`

---
